# Review: balance pass v0.26 and "no hardcoded values" (read-only)

You are a second opinion for the architect of **Glyphbound**, a first-person shooter / RPG hybrid made in a 4-day game jam (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++, in `Glyphbound/`). **Do not edit any file, do not build, do not run Unreal.** You may read files and run `git diff` and `node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js`. Answer in English, short and concrete.

Nothing of this is compiled or play-tested yet: the architect wants your notes **before** the single build, so that everything can be changed at once.

## The owner's brief (translated from Spanish)
"Balance, with maths, the numbers of the whole game, except the exact percentages of the skill-tree upgrades (leave those, suggestions are welcome): the player's attributes (health, mana), spell costs, spell damage, cooldowns, weapon and bullet damage, resistances and weaknesses. Not everything is implemented, so use placeholders; no enemy is final yet. We want fights a little long but not too long. The difficulty of a fast-paced shooter: in the waves enemies are always dying. No bullet-sponge enemies, unless it is an enemy with low damage that is slow. There are two bosses (the medieval Valkyrie and the cyberpunk Valkyrie) and then a final boss, where the player has every tool. There are no levels. The player must NOT get enough skill points for the whole tree: they pick branches for a build, but enough to be well armed as a mage with the equipped spells, and to have at least one gun and a half well upgraded."
Later the owner approved: waves of 60-90 s, Valkyries of about 2 min, final boss about 3 min, 36 skill points in total, and asked: "not a single hardcoded value; everything must be easy to change while testing".

## What to read
1. `Design/GDD.md`, section 19 "Balance" (the method and every table) and section 6 "Elemental build-up".
2. The data: `Glyphbound/Content/Data/Player.json`, `Weapons.json`, `Spells.json`, `Enemies.json`, `Encounters.json`, `StatusEffects.json`, `Items.json`, `Game.json`, `Skills.json`, `QTE.json`. `git diff HEAD -- Glyphbound/Content/Data` shows what changed.
3. `node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js` prints the tables (its assumptions are in the `ASSUME` block at the top of the script).
4. `git diff HEAD -- Glyphbound/Source` is the uncommitted C++ change (an implementer moved the last hardcoded gameplay numbers into the data files; its brief is `Prompts/balance1_no_hardcoded.md`). It must keep today's behavior exactly.

Facts about the game code that matter for the maths: the QTE maps its score linearly from a param's `Min` (score 0) to `Max` (score 1), `Base` is used only with the QTE off; a skill perk with `Multiply` stacks linearly with its rank (1.1 at rank 3 = x1.30); area spells tick with one timer per spell actor (an enemy must be inside at the tick); modifiers combine as (base + adds) x product of multipliers; a gun's enchant multiplies its damage by 1 + 0.25 x strength (strength 0.6-1.4); enemy stat blocks marked sub-boss / Valkyrie / Entity run on the generic melee or ranged enemy AI for now.

## What I want from you
**Part A, balance (most important).** Check the numbers against the brief and against each other. Look for:
- arithmetic or modelling mistakes in the method (GDD 19 "Method") or in `balance_model.js`, and assumptions that are clearly off for this kind of game;
- outliers: a weapon, spell, enemy or perk combination that dominates or is useless (work out the strongest builds reachable with 36 points and say what they do to the fight lengths; check the multiplicative stacking of Heavy Rounds, Quick Trigger, Marksman, Deadeye, the enchant and weak points);
- degenerate play: infinite loops of control (stun / freeze / slow / ice wall / ice shield uptime with the cooldown and cost perks), unlimited sustain (regeneration, Precision Recovery, potions), ammo or mana that never runs out or always runs out, a boss that cannot be finished with the resources a player carries;
- whether fight lengths, time-to-kill per enemy template and the time the player survives fit "fast shooter, hard, enemies always dying, no sponges";
- whether resistances and weaknesses make any enemy unfair or trivial;
- whether the 36-point budget really buys "a well-armed mage plus one gun and a half".
For every problem give the concrete number you would change, from what to what, and one line of reason. Do not rewrite the design.

**Part B, code diff.** Only real problems: anything that will not compile on UE 5.9, any change of behavior with the default values, a key read under one name in C++ and written under another in JSON, a value read once that `gb.ReloadData` will not refresh, a literal from the brief that was not moved.

## Format
A prioritized list, at most 15 items in total, each: **[A or B] [high / medium / low]** what, where (file and key or line), the change you propose, why. Then one line: what you checked and found fine. No preamble.

// balance_model.js - reads Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json and prints the balance tables.
// Usage (from the repo root):  node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js
// It changes nothing: it is the calculator behind the numbers in Design/GDD.md section 19.
// Every assumption of the model is in the ASSUME block below.
'use strict';
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const dataDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'Glyphbound', 'Content', 'Data');
const load = (name) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dataDir, name), 'utf8'));

const player = load('Player.json');
const weapons = load('Weapons.json').Weapons;
const spells = load('Spells.json').Spells;
const enemies = load('Enemies.json').Enemies;
const skills = load('Skills.json').Skills;
const encounters = load('Encounters.json').Encounters;
const effects = load('StatusEffects.json').StatusEffects;
const items = load('Items.json').Items;
const game = load('Game.json').Values;

// ---- Model assumptions (not game data) ----
const ASSUME = {
	Accuracy: 0.7,        // share of shots that hit (default; per weapon below)
	WeaponAccuracy: { Rifle: 0.6, Shotgun: 0.75, Pistol: 0.75 }, // automatic fire and recoil miss more
	Uptime: 0.7,          // share of the fight spent attacking (the rest: dodging, switching, QTE)
	HeadShare: 0.25,      // share of the hits that land on the head
	QtePower: 0.75,       // typical QTE score; the game maps 0 -> Min and 1 -> Max in a straight line (Base is the value with the QTE off)
	AvoidMelee: 0.85,     // share of melee damage a moving player avoids (the player is much faster than most enemies)
	AvoidRanged: 0.6,     // share of enemy shots a moving player dodges
	SkillPointBudget: 36, // points the player should own before the final boss
	WaveOverhead: 6,      // seconds lost per sub-wave: spawn delay, enemies walking in, finding targets
	BuildMult: 1.3,       // gun damage of a mid-game build over the base gun (perks, enchant)
	// Target seconds of real fight per archetype (used to print "target health").
	BossSeconds: { SubBoss: 50, ValkyriePast: 120, ValkyrieFuture: 120, Entity: 180 }
};

const pad = (s, n) => String(s).padEnd(n);
const num = (v, d = 1) => (Math.round(v * Math.pow(10, d)) / Math.pow(10, d)).toString();
const line = (cols, widths) => console.log(cols.map((c, i) => pad(c, widths[i])).join(' '));
const title = (t) => console.log('\n=== ' + t + ' ===');

// ---- Weapons ----
function weaponRow(w) {
	const s = w.Stats;
	const pellets = s.Pellets || 1;
	const near = s.FalloffNearMult === undefined ? 1 : s.FalloffNearMult;
	const far = s.FalloffFarMult === undefined ? 1 : s.FalloffFarMult;
	const best = Math.max(near, far);
	const hit = s.Damage * pellets * best;
	const burst = hit / s.FireInterval;
	let sustained = burst;
	let carried = Infinity;
	if (s.MagazineSize) {
		const cycle = s.MagazineSize * s.FireInterval + s.ReloadTime;
		sustained = hit * s.MagazineSize / cycle;
		carried = hit * s.MagazineSize * (1 + (s.SpareMagazines || 0));
	}
	const head = s.HeadshotMult || 1;
	const accuracy = ASSUME.WeaponAccuracy[w.Id] || ASSUME.Accuracy;
	const expected = sustained * accuracy * ASSUME.Uptime * (1 + ASSUME.HeadShare * (head - 1));
	const nearShare = near / best;
	const farShare = far / best;
	const buildUp = Math.max(s.FireBuildUp || 0, s.LightningBuildUp || 0, s.IceBuildUp || 0);
	return { id: w.Id, hit, burst, sustained, expected, carried, head, accuracy, nearSustained: sustained * nearShare, farSustained: sustained * farShare, buildUpPerSecond: buildUp / s.FireInterval, magDamage: s.MagazineSize ? hit * s.MagazineSize : 0 };
}
const weaponRows = weapons.map(weaponRow);

title('Weapons (at their best range, no perks)');
let W = [18, 8, 8, 10, 12, 10, 9, 6, 9];
line(['Weapon', 'Hit', 'Burst', 'Sustained', 'Near / far', 'Expected', 'Carried', 'Head', 'BuildUp/s'], W);
for (const r of weaponRows) {
	line([r.id, num(r.hit), num(r.burst), num(r.sustained), num(r.nearSustained, 0) + ' / ' + num(r.farSustained, 0), num(r.expected), r.carried === Infinity ? 'free' : num(r.carried, 0), 'x' + r.head, num(r.buildUpPerSecond)], W);
}
console.log('Hit, Burst, Sustained: at the best range, every shot hitting the body. Sustained includes reloads. Expected: with accuracy, uptime and head share.');

// ---- Enemies ----
const fighters = enemies.filter((e) => e.AttackMode !== 'None');
title('Enemies');
W = [22, 8, 7, 7, 9, 9, 12];
line(['Enemy', 'Health', 'Speed', 'Hit', 'Interval', 'DPS', 'HitsToKillPlayer'], W);
for (const e of fighters) {
	const s = e.Stats;
	line([e.Id, s.MaxHealth, s.MoveSpeed, s.AttackDamage, s.AttackInterval, num(s.AttackDamage / s.AttackInterval), Math.ceil(player.Stats.MaxHealth / s.AttackDamage)], W);
}

title('Hits to kill, body / head (no perks, best range), and seconds of burst fire on the body');
W = [22].concat(weaponRows.map(() => 20));
line(['Enemy'].concat(weaponRows.map((r) => r.id)), W);
for (const e of fighters) {
	const hp = e.Stats.MaxHealth;
	line([e.Id].concat(weaponRows.map((r) => {
		const body = Math.ceil(hp / r.hit);
		const headHits = Math.ceil(hp / (r.hit * r.head));
		return body + ' / ' + headHits + '  (' + num(hp / r.burst) + ' s)';
	})), W);
}

// ---- Ammo economy ----
title('Ammo economy (expected ammo damage returned per kill / spent per kill; target 0.6-0.7 future, about half in the past)');
const ammoItem = items.find((i) => i.Id === 'Ammo_Magazine');
const magsPerPickup = ammoItem && ammoItem.Stats ? ammoItem.Stats.SpareMagazines : 1;
const guns = weaponRows.filter((r) => r.magDamage > 0);
W = [22].concat(guns.map(() => 12));
line(['Enemy'].concat(guns.map((g) => g.id)), W);
for (const e of fighters) {
	const drop = (e.Drops || []).filter((d) => d.ItemId === 'Ammo_Magazine').reduce((a, d) => a + d.Count * d.Chance, 0);
	line([e.Id].concat(guns.map((g) => num(drop * magsPerPickup * g.magDamage / (e.Stats.MaxHealth / ASSUME.Accuracy), 2))), W);
}

// ---- Spells ----
title('Spells (damage on ONE target; Min / Base / Max by QTE; damage per mana at Base)');
W = [16, 6, 9, 22, 12, 22];
line(['Spell', 'Mana', 'Cooldown', 'Damage', 'Dmg/mana', 'Notes'], W);
for (const sp of spells) {
	const p = sp.Params;
	if (sp.bPassive || !p.ManaCost) { continue; }
	const mana = p.ManaCost.Base;
	let dmg = null;
	let note = '';
	let cost = mana;
	if (p.Damage) {
		dmg = [p.Damage.Min, p.Damage.Base, p.Damage.Max];
	} else if (p.DamagePerSecond && p.DamagePerSecond.Base > 0 && p.ManaPerSecond) {
		const seconds = (player.Stats.MaxMana - mana) / p.ManaPerSecond.Base;
		dmg = [p.DamagePerSecond.Min, p.DamagePerSecond.Base, p.DamagePerSecond.Max].map((d) => d * seconds);
		cost = player.Stats.MaxMana;
		note = 'full mana bar, ' + num(seconds) + ' s';
	} else if (p.DamagePerSecond && p.DamagePerSecond.Base > 0 && p.Duration) {
		dmg = [p.DamagePerSecond.Min * p.Duration.Min, p.DamagePerSecond.Base * p.Duration.Base, p.DamagePerSecond.Max * p.Duration.Max];
		note = 'if the enemy stays inside';
	}
	if (p.Duration && p.Duration.Base > 0 && p.Cooldown.Base > 0 && sp.Id !== 'LightningRune') {
		note += (note ? '; ' : '') + 'uptime ' + num(100 * p.Duration.Base / p.Cooldown.Base, 0) + '% (' + num(100 * p.Duration.Max / p.Cooldown.Base, 0) + '% perfect)';
	}
	line([sp.Id, mana, p.Cooldown.Base, dmg ? dmg.map((d) => num(d, 0)).join(' / ') : '-', dmg ? num(dmg[1] / cost, 2) : '-', note], W);
}

// ---- Mana ----
title('Mana');
const ps = player.Stats;
console.log('Pool ' + ps.MaxMana + ', regen ' + ps.ManaRegen + '/s after ' + ps.ManaRegenDelay + ' s. Empty to full: ' + num(ps.MaxMana / ps.ManaRegen + ps.ManaRegenDelay) + ' s.');
console.log('Mana over a 75 s wave (regen running ' + num(ASSUME.Uptime * 100, 0) + '% of the time): ' + num(ps.MaxMana + ps.ManaRegen * ASSUME.Uptime * 75, 0));

// ---- Player survival ----
title('Player survival');
console.log('Health ' + ps.MaxHealth + ', regen ' + ps.HealthRegen + '/s after ' + ps.HealthRegenDelay + ' s without damage.');
for (const e of fighters) {
	const share = 100 * e.Stats.AttackDamage / ps.MaxHealth;
	console.log(pad(e.Id, 22) + ' one hit = ' + num(share, 0) + '% of max health' + (share >= 30 ? '  (strong hit: interrupts the flamethrower)' : ''));
}

// ---- Elemental build-up ----
title('Elemental build-up: hits to fill the bar (threshold x build-up taken), per enemy');
const elems = ['Fire', 'Lightning', 'Ice'];
for (const e of fighters) {
	const parts = elems.map((el) => {
		const th = e.Stats[el + 'BuildUpThreshold'] || game['ElementalDefault.' + el + 'BuildUpThreshold'];
		const taken = e.Stats[el + 'BuildUpTaken'] === undefined ? game['ElementalDefault.' + el + 'BuildUpTaken'] : e.Stats[el + 'BuildUpTaken'];
		const dmg = e.Stats[el + 'DamageTaken'] === undefined ? game['ElementalDefault.' + el + 'DamageTaken'] : e.Stats[el + 'DamageTaken'];
		return el + ': threshold ' + th + ', build-up x' + taken + ', damage x' + dmg;
	});
	console.log(pad(e.Id, 22) + parts.join(' | '));
}
const ignite = effects.find((f) => f.Id === 'Ignite');
if (ignite) {
	const total = -ignite.HealthPerTick * (ignite.Duration / ignite.TickInterval);
	console.log('Ignite base damage: ' + num(total, 0) + ' over ' + ignite.Duration + ' s (times each enemy\'s IgniteDamageMult).');
	for (const e of fighters) {
		const mult = e.Stats.IgniteDamageMult === undefined ? game['ElementalDefault.IgniteDamageMult'] : e.Stats.IgniteDamageMult;
		console.log('  ' + pad(e.Id, 22) + num(100 * total * mult / e.Stats.MaxHealth, 1) + '% of its health');
	}
}

// ---- Boss health targets ----
title('Boss health targets (expected damage per second of a build x target seconds)');
const bestGun = guns.reduce((a, g) => Math.max(a, g.expected), 0);
const staff = weaponRows.find((r) => r.id === 'Staff_Apprentice');
const spellDps = ps.ManaRegen * ASSUME.Uptime * 3.5; // about 3.5 damage per mana at a typical QTE
const stages = [
	['SubBoss', 1.10, 'about 8 points spent'],
	['ValkyriePast', 1.45, 'about 24 points spent'],
	['ValkyrieFuture', 1.60, 'about 29 points spent'],
	['Entity', 1.75, 'about 34 points spent, sword owned']
];
for (const [id, mult, why] of stages) {
	const dps = bestGun * mult + spellDps;
	console.log(pad(id, 16) + 'expected ' + num(dps, 0) + ' dmg/s (' + why + ') x ' + ASSUME.BossSeconds[id] + ' s = ' + num(dps * ASSUME.BossSeconds[id], 0) + ' health');
}
console.log('(best gun expected ' + num(bestGun) + ', spells add about ' + num(spellDps) + ', apprentice staff alone ' + num(staff ? staff.expected : 0) + ')');
for (const e of fighters) {
	const dps = bestGun * 1.1 + spellDps;
	console.log('  ' + pad(e.Id, 22) + 'real time to kill at sub-boss stage: ' + num(e.Stats.MaxHealth / dps) + ' s');
}

// ---- Skill points ----
title('Skill points');
const treeCost = (tree) => skills.filter((s) => s.Tree === tree).reduce((a, s) => a + s.Cost * s.MaxRank, 0);
const total = treeCost('Weapon') + treeCost('Spell');
console.log('Weapon tree: ' + treeCost('Weapon') + ' points. Spell tree: ' + treeCost('Spell') + ' points. Both: ' + total + '.');
const real = encounters.filter((e) => e.Id.indexOf('Test_') !== 0);
const fromEncounters = real.reduce((a, e) => a + (e.SkillPoints || 0), 0);
const sum = player.StartingSkillPoints + fromEncounters;
console.log('Starting points: ' + player.StartingSkillPoints + '. Encounters (without Test_*) give: ' + fromEncounters + '. Sum: ' + sum + ' (budget ' + ASSUME.SkillPointBudget + ' = ' + num(100 * ASSUME.SkillPointBudget / total, 0) + '% of both trees)' + (sum === ASSUME.SkillPointBudget ? ' OK' : '  <-- OFF BUDGET'));
console.log('A Multiply perk stacks in a straight line with its rank: 1.1 at rank 3 is x1.30.');

title('Encounters (health per wave; raw and expected incoming damage per second with every enemy of the wave attacking)');
const enemyById = {};
for (const e of enemies) { enemyById[e.Id] = e; }
W = [18, 4, 7, 8, 9, 44];
const fightDps = bestGun * ASSUME.BuildMult + spellDps;
line(['Encounter', 'SP', 'Kills', 'Health', 'Seconds', 'Per wave: kills, health, raw dmg/s -> expected dmg/s (seconds to die)'], W);
for (const enc of encounters) {
	let kills = 0;
	let health = 0;
	const parts = [];
	for (const wave of enc.Waves) {
		let wk = 0, wh = 0, raw = 0, exp = 0;
		for (const sp of wave.Spawns) {
			const e = enemyById[sp.EnemyId];
			if (!e) { console.log('  UNKNOWN ENEMY ' + sp.EnemyId + ' in ' + enc.Id); continue; }
			const dps = (e.Stats.AttackDamage || 0) / (e.Stats.AttackInterval || 1);
			wk += sp.Count; wh += sp.Count * e.Stats.MaxHealth; raw += sp.Count * dps;
			exp += sp.Count * dps * (1 - (e.AttackMode === 'Ranged' ? ASSUME.AvoidRanged : ASSUME.AvoidMelee));
		}
		kills += wk; health += wh;
		parts.push(wk + 'k ' + wh + 'hp ' + num(raw, 0) + '->' + num(exp, 1) + ' (' + num(ps.MaxHealth / Math.max(exp, 0.01), 0) + ' s)');
	}
	line([enc.Id, enc.SkillPoints, kills, health, num(health / fightDps + enc.Waves.length * ASSUME.WaveOverhead, 0), parts.join(' | ')], W);
}
console.log('Seconds = health / ' + num(fightDps, 0) + ' expected dmg/s (mid-game build) + ' + ASSUME.WaveOverhead + ' s per sub-wave. Targets: waves 60-90, sub-bosses 40-60, Valkyries about 120, Entity about 180.');
# Task: move the last hardcoded gameplay numbers into the data files

Project: **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++), in `Glyphbound/`. Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `Prompts/implementer_rules.md` first. In this task you **do not build, do not run tests, do not open the Unreal Editor and do not commit**: the architect reviews your diff, asks a second reviewer, and then builds once. So write code that compiles: check every engine API you are not sure about in `C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9\Engine\Source`.

Owner's request (translated): "Try to leave not a single hardcoded value, because we are going to test all these values and they must be easy to change." The testers change numbers in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` and run `gb.ReloadData` in the console; nothing may need a recompile.

An audit found the literals below. For each one: read it from the data with the helper that file already uses (`UGBDataSubsystem::GetGameValue`, `UGBStatsComponent::GetStat`, the player's `ReadStat`, the QTE component's `ReadStat`, `ReadItemStat`, `FGBSpellCast::Get` / `AGBSpellActor::ReadParam`), keep **today's value as the code default**, and **add the key with today's value to the JSON file**, so the game plays exactly the same after your change. Line numbers are from today's tree and may be off by a few lines.

**Rules for the JSON files:** the architect has just rebalanced them. **Only add new keys. Never change, reorder or reformat an existing value or line.** Edit them as text (keep tabs and the one-line objects as they are). New stat-name constants go next to the similar ones (`Core/GBStatNames.h` or the header that already holds that file's names).

Read the value where it is used (live), not once in a constructor or `BeginPlay`, unless reading it per frame would be wasteful; in that case re-read it on `OnDataReloaded`.

## 1. `Game.json` "Values"
| Key | Today | Where |
|---|---|---|
| `FrostSlowMax` | 0.95 | `Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.cpp:16` (used near :314) |
| `KnockbackMinLift` | 0.35 | `Combat/GBProjectile.cpp:27` (used near :364) |
| `ProjectileMaxLifetime` | 10 | `Combat/GBProjectile.h:34` `MaxLifetime` default; also the `10` inside `max(10, Range/Speed + 1.0)` in `Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.cpp:10,:40` |
| `SpellProjectileLifetimeMargin` | 1.0 | the `+ 1.0` in that same expression |
| `EnemyMeleeAcceptanceFraction` | 0.5 | `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:442` (`AttackRange * 0.5f`) |
| `EnemyProjectileRangeMult` | 1.5 | `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:773` (`AggroRange * 1.5f`) |
| `EnemyAimHeightFraction` | 0.5 | `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:38` `ChestHeightFraction` |
| `EnemyStuckSpeedFraction` | 0.25 | `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:40` |
| `EnemySteerSweepRadiusScale` | 0.9 | `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:39` |
| `DuplicateGunMagazines` | 1 | `World/GBPickup.cpp:195` (`AddSpareMagazines(1)`) |
| `DropScatterRadius` | 90 | `World/GBPickup.cpp:25` |
| `TestSkillPointAmount` | 1 | `UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp:829` (`AddPoints(1)`, the test button) |
| `RespawnHealthFraction`, `RespawnManaFraction`, `RespawnRefillAmmo` | 1, 1, 1 | checkpoint respawn, `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.cpp` near :576-580 and :840 |
| `EraTravelHealthFraction`, `EraTravelManaFraction`, `EraTravelRefillAmmo` | 1, 1, 1 | era travel, `World/GBEraSubsystem.cpp:196` |

For the six restore keys: on respawn the player is set to `fraction x max` (a respawn always starts from that value); on era travel health and mana are raised to **at least** `fraction x max` and never lowered. `...RefillAmmo` is 1 = refill every gun as today, 0 = leave the ammo as it is. If today's code is one shared "restore everything" function, give it parameters instead of copying it.

Also: `ElementalTickInterval` is read once in `BeginPlay` (`Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.cpp:53`), so `gb.ReloadData` does not change it. Make it follow a reload.

## 2. `Player.json` "Stats"
| Key | Today | Where |
|---|---|---|
| `AccelerationPerSpeed` | 10 | `Player/GBPlayerCharacter.cpp:50` |
| `BrakingPerSpeed` | 8 | `:51` |
| `GroundFriction` | 8 | `:52` (`GroundFrictionValue`) |
| `InteractFacingMinDot` | 0.3 | `:48` |
| `LookSensitivity`, `StickYawRate`, `StickPitchRate`, `PitchLimit` | 2.5, 200, 140, 89 | `:40-44` (check the real names and values there and keep them) |
| `DashExitSpeedMult` | 1 | `:389`: speed kept after a dash = direction x walk speed x this |

## 3. Enemies: one new optional stat
`ProjectileGravity` (default 0) for enemy shots, read next to `ProjectileSpeed` / `ProjectileRadius` in `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:768-773` and passed to the projectile. Add `"ProjectileGravity": 0` to every enemy in `Enemies.json` whose `AttackMode` is `Ranged`.

## 4. Electric grenade (`Items.json`, `ElectricGrenade` "Stats"; code in `Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp:260-280`, `UseThrowable`)
Today the grenade always uses linear blast falloff, never pushes, and bounces with the engine defaults. Read and add: `ExplosionFalloff` 1 (1 = linear falloff, 0 = full damage in the whole radius, same meaning as the spell param), `ExplosionKnockback` 0, `Bounciness` and `Friction` (today's values are the `UProjectileMovementComponent` defaults: look them up in the engine source and write those numbers). `Range` is already in the file.

## 5. Spells (`Spells.json` "Params", each `{ "Base": x, "Min": x, "Max": x, "ScaledBy": "None" }`)
- `TouchMargin` 15 on `FireWall` and `IceWall`: `Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:18` `WallTouchMarginCm` (used near :141).
- `BlockArcDegrees` 180 on `IceShield`: `Spells/GBSpell_Shield.cpp:22` blocks when `Dot >= 0`, i.e. a 180 degree arc in front. Block when the angle between the shield's forward and the direction to the attacker is at most half this arc (compare the dot with `cos(arc/2)`; 180 must behave exactly as today, 360 blocks from every side).

## 6. QTE (`QTE.json` "Stats"; code in `Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.cpp` near :426-507)
Today a spell that fizzles (every QTE step missed) still costs its full mana and starts its full cooldown. Add:
- `QTE.FailManaRefund` 0: share of the mana cost given back when the spell fizzles (0 = today, 1 = full refund).
- `QTE.FailCooldownMult` 1: the cooldown of a fizzled spell is multiplied by this (1 = today, 0 = no cooldown).
Do not change what happens when the QTE itself refuses to start (that refund stays).

## Do NOT touch
- `Weapons/GBGun.*`, `Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.*`, `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (another task is working there).
- `Tests/`, `World/GBTestArena.cpp`, `Glyphbound.Build.cs`, `Config/`.
- Guards and engineering limits (minimum tick interval, clamps of angles, array sizes, `NumSlots`), UI layout numbers, the visual constants of the explosion effect, the QTE gesture maths.
- No new tests: these are plumbing changes with unchanged behavior.

-float AGBSpell_Shield::ComputeBlockedDamage(float Amount, const FVector& HitDirection, const FVector& AimDirection, float InBlockFraction, float ShieldHealthLeft)
+float AGBSpell_Shield::ComputeBlockedDamage(float Amount, const FVector& HitDirection, const FVector& AimDirection, float InBlockFraction, float ShieldHealthLeft, float BlockArcDegrees)
 {
 	if (Amount <= 0.f || ShieldHealthLeft <= 0.f || HitDirection.IsNearlyZero() || AimDirection.IsNearlyZero())
 	{
 		return 0.f;
 	}
-	// A hit from the front travels against the aim (source within 90 degrees of the aim direction).
-	if (FVector::DotProduct(HitDirection.GetSafeNormal(), AimDirection.GetSafeNormal()) >= 0.f)
+	const float ClampedArc = FMath::Clamp(BlockArcDegrees, 0.f, 360.f);
+	if (ClampedArc <= 0.f)
 	{
 		return 0.f;
 	}
+	if (ClampedArc < 360.f)
+	{
+		// A hit blocks when the direction to the attacker is within half the arc of the aim: the dot of the
+		// hit travel direction (source to caster) with the aim is then below minus the cosine of half the arc.
+		// cos(half of 180) is snapped to 0, so 180 behaves exactly like the old "Dot >= 0" front rule.
+		const float CosHalf = FMath::IsNearlyEqual(ClampedArc, 180.f) ? 0.f : FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ClampedArc * 0.5f));
+		if (FVector::DotProduct(HitDirection.GetSafeNormal(), AimDirection.GetSafeNormal()) >= -CosHalf)
+		{
+			return 0.f;
+		}
+	}
 	return FMath::Min(Amount * FMath::Clamp(InBlockFraction, 0.f, 1.f), ShieldHealthLeft);
 }

+		float CooldownMult = 1.f;
+		if (UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
+		{
+			RefundFraction = FMath::Clamp(QTE->GetFailManaRefund(), 0.f, 1.f);
+			CooldownMult = FMath::Max(0.f, QTE->GetFailCooldownMult());
+		}
+		StartCooldown(SpellId, FullCooldown * CooldownMult);
+		if (RefundFraction > 0.f)
+		{
+			if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
+			{
+				Attributes->RestoreMana(GetManaCost(SpellId) * RefundFraction);
+			}
+		}
 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::QTEFailed);
 		return;
 	}
 
+	StartCooldown(SpellId, FullCooldown);
+
 	FGBSpellCast SpellCast = GBSpell::ResolveCast(*Definition, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats);
 	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
 	SpellCast.Caster = Owner;

-void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
+void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll(float HealthFraction, float ManaFraction, bool bOnlyRaise)
 {
 	bDead = false;
-	Health = GetMaxHealth();
-	Mana = GetMaxMana();

+	const float MaxHealth = GetMaxHealth();
+	const float MaxMana = GetMaxMana();
+	const float WantHealth = FMath::Clamp(HealthFraction, 0.f, 1.f) * MaxHealth;
+	const float WantMana = FMath::Clamp(ManaFraction, 0.f, 1.f) * MaxMana;
+	// Respawn always starts from the fractions; era travel only raises towards them, never lowers.
+	Health = bOnlyRaise ? FMath::Max(Health, WantHealth) : WantHealth;
+	Mana = bOnlyRaise ? FMath::Max(Mana, WantMana) : WantMana;
+	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, MaxHealth);
+	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, MaxMana);
 }

 succeeded in 1321ms:
- **Enemies:** resistances, weaknesses and thresholds per enemy are in Â§19 (v0.26).
- **Skill trees:** 6 nodes each, 1-2 points per rank, up to 3 ranks.
- Every spell number in `Spells.json`; the v0.26 values and their reasons are in Â§19.

## 19. Balance (v0.26, architect, 2026-11-12)
The owner's brief: fights a little long but not too long; a fast shooter where enemies die all the time in the waves; no bullet sponges, except an enemy that is slow and hits softly; two Valkyries and then a final boss fought with every tool; no levels; not enough skill points for the whole tree, but enough for a well-armed mage plus one gun and a half well upgraded. **Every number here is provisional and lives in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json`**; nothing needs a recompile (edit the file, run `gb.ReloadData`). `node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js` prints every table below from the data, so after changing a number the tables can be checked again in a second.

### Method
- **Three anchors.** The player has 100 health and 100 mana for the whole game. The reference damage is the base pistol on the body: 80 per second. An enemy's health is "seconds it should last x reference damage".
- **Real damage is about half the paper damage:** the model takes 70% of the time attacking, 60-75% of the shots hitting and a quarter of the hits on the head. With that, each gun gives about 40 real damage per second without perks, a mid-game build about 70 with its spells, and a full build about 90.
- **The QTE maps its score in a straight line from Min (every step missed but one) to Max (perfect).** `Base` is the value with the QTE switched off. Min is half of Base and Max is one and a half for damage, so a careful cast is worth three sloppy ones.
- **Skill perks stack in a straight line:** +10% per rank is +30% at rank 3.

### Player
| | Value | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Health | 100, +3/s after 6 s without damage | No regeneration inside a fight; a clean disengage (dash, ice wall) is rewarded |
| Mana | 100, +6/s after 1.5 s | Five spells in a burst, then about one every 3 s; a 75 s wave gives about 400 mana |
| Largest single hit in the game | 32 (sub-bosses) | Never more than 45% of the health bar: no one-shots. 30% or more counts as a "strong hit" and stops the flamethrower |

### Weapons (no perks)
| Weapon | Hit | Sustained damage/s (with reloads) | Carried damage | Role |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Apprentice staff | 12 every 0.35 s | 34 | free | The fallback that never runs out: about 55% of a gun |
| Ember staff | 22 every 0.6 s | 37 + Ignite | free | Slower, sets enemies on fire |
| Pistol | 20 every 0.25 s | 61 (121 on the head) | 2,100 | Precision: the best head bonus (x2) |
| Shotgun | 8 x 10 every 0.8 s | 71 up close, 14 far | 2,400 | Risk: one shell kills a soldier at arm's length |
| Rifle | 12 every 0.1 s | 60 near, 86 far | 2,480 | Range and volume; less accurate, weak up close |
| Elemental sword | 50 every 0.5 s | 100 | free | Late game, melee risk, hits a 90 degree arc |

The three guns come out even (about 40 real damage per second each). The rifle was the outlier before (140 burst against 82 and 94).

**Ammo:** each gun carries about 2,100-2,500 damage, about one long wave shooting only. Enemies of the future return 50-95% of the ammo spent on them, enemies of the past about half of that, so the past pushes the player to the staff and spells and nobody runs dry (the staff is free). Drop chance of a magazine: swarm 8%, soldier 20%, elite 45%, tank 100% (+30% a second one), sub-boss 3 magazines; half of that in the past.

### Spells (Base; damage Min/Max is 50% / 150%)
| Spell | Mana | Cooldown | Effect | Damage per mana (one target) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Fireball | 15 | 1.5 s | 40 in a 2 m blast | 2.7, times the enemies caught |
| Lightning bolt | 10 | 0.8 s | 30, goes through enemies | 3.0 |
| Lightning rune | 20 | 6 s | 80 in a 3 m blast | 4.0 (the best: it needs setting up) |
| Flamethrower | 10 + 15/s | 6 s | 50/s in a short cone | 3.0, a full bar is 300 per enemy |
| Fire wall | 25 | 10 s | 60/s for 6 s, ticks every 0.25 s | A soldier crossing it loses a quarter to a half of its health |
| Static orb | 25 | 7 s | 35/s within 2.5 m, travels at 3 m/s for 6 s | About 60 to each enemy it passes |
| Ice wall | 20 | 12 s | Blocks for 8 s (5-11) | Up 67% of the time, 92% with a perfect QTE |
| Ice shield | 20 | 18 s | 12 s (8-16), 80 health (40-120), blocks 75% from the front | Up 67% of the time |
| Ice floor | 20 | 10 s | 50% slow for 6 s | Up 60% of the time |
| Weapon enchant | 20 | 18 s | +25% gun damage (15-35%) for 15 s (8-22), element build-up | Costs about a fifth of the mana regeneration |

Rules behind them: a direct spell gives about 3 damage per mana; a spell that needs setup gives more; area spells give less per target because they hit several. **No control spell can be up all the time without a perfect QTE** (before, the ice wall and the ice shield had a cooldown shorter than their duration). The fire wall ticked every 0.5 s and an orc crossed it in 0.4 s without being touched; it now ticks every 0.25 s and is a little thicker.

### Enemy templates
| Template | Past (pixel art) | Future (3D) | Health | Speed | Hit | Lasts |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Swarm | Castle Imp | Scrap Drone | 30 | 600 | 6 | 2 pistol shots |
| Soldier | Castle Orc (melee), Orc Shaman (ranged) | Armored Orc (ranged) | 50-70 | 380-450 | 8-10 | 3-4 pistol shots, 1 shell |
| Elite | Orc Champion | Orc Enforcer (ranged) | 180 | 380-400 | 16-18 | about 3 s |
| Slow tank (the one sponge) | Orc Juggernaut | Siege Orc | 480 | 260 | 20 every 2.2 s | about 8 s |
| Sub-boss | Orc Brute | Orc Warlord | 2,400 / 3,000 | 340-360 | 32 | 40-50 s |
| Boss | Valkyrie (medieval) | Valkyrie (cyberpunk) | 9,000 / 10,000 | 480 | 18-22 | about 2 min |
| Final boss | The Entity, two phases of 8,000 | | 16,000 | 420-520 | 20-26 | about 3 min |

The player (750) is faster than everything, so melee enemies only hit a player who stops; the pressure comes from numbers, ranged enemies and the swarm. The tank is the only sponge and it is the slowest and softest hitter for its size. Bosses and sub-bosses are stat blocks for the generic enemy until their own behavior exists; their heavy attacks, when written, should stay at 40 or less.

**A boss cannot be killed with ammo alone:** a full ammo load is about a third of a Valkyrie's health, the mana of the fight another quarter, and the staff or the sword the rest. That is the "combine both eras" pillar in numbers. Boss arenas should still offer a magazine now and then.

### Resistances and weaknesses
- **Weakness:** +25% damage and +50% build-up of that element. **Resistance:** -25% damage and -50% build-up. An enemy never resists two elements; bosses change between phases.
- **Past (flesh): weak to fire.** **Future (armor, electronics): weak to lightning, resists fire.** The swarm of the past is weak to ice (a freeze stops a rush). The Orc Champion also resists ice; the Juggernaut resists lightning.
- **Orc Brute:** resists fire, weak to ice. **Valkyrie, medieval (bound by runes):** resists lightning, weak to ice. **Valkyrie, cyberpunk (cryogenic tube):** resists ice, weak to fire. **Entity:** phase 1 (the machine) weak to lightning and resists fire; phase 2 (the ghost) weak to fire and resists ice.
- **Build-up threshold by template:** swarm 60, soldier 100, elite 150, tank 200, sub-boss 250, boss 400. Tanks, sub-bosses and bosses are never stunned or frozen: Shock only interrupts their attack and Frost only slows them (40% tanks and sub-bosses, 25% bosses), with longer immunity after each status.
- **Ignite scales with the template** (`IgniteDamageMult`: tank x2, sub-boss x4, boss x8), so it is about 15-20% of an elite or a tank and 3-4% of a boss per proc instead of nothing.

### Encounters and length
Sub-waves of 5 to 10 enemies, so something dies every 2-3 seconds. With every enemy of a sub-wave attacking, a player who avoids most of it (85% of melee, 60% of shots) takes 8-15 damage per second: 7 to 13 seconds to die without healing or defending. That is the "hard" setting; the levers are the counts in `Encounters.json`.

| Encounter | Enemies | Health | Estimated length |
|---|---|---|---|
| Opening fights | 8 | 400-500 | about 20 s |
| Waves 1 to 3 of each era | 27-31 in four sub-waves | 1,600 -> 2,900 | 50 -> 65 s |
| Elemental zones | 28-36 in four sub-waves | 2,500-2,700 | about 60 s |
| Sub-bosses (with a small escort) | 4-5 | 2,600-3,200 | 45-55 s |
| Valkyries | 1 | 9,000 / 10,000 | about 2 min |
| Entity | 2 phases | 16,000 | about 3 min |

`Encounters.json` now holds one ready-made encounter of each kind (placeholders for the level designers: `Opening_*`, `Wave_Past_1..3`, `Wave_Future_1..3`, `SubBoss_*`, `Zone_Fire/Lightning/Ice/Air`, `Valkyrie_*`, `Entity`), besides the two test waves of the arena.

### Skill points
Both trees cost 75 points (weapons 33, spells 42). **Budget: 36 points before the Entity (48%).** That buys, for example, one gun fully specialized and a second one half-way (about 15 points) plus three or four spells upgraded and cheaper casting (about 15), with 6 left over: a build, not the whole tree.

| Source | Points |
|---|---|
| Start | 2 |
| Each of the two opening fights | 1 |
| Each wave (6) | 1 |
| Each sub-boss (2) | 2 |
| Each elemental zone (4) | 3 |
| Each Valkyrie | 5 |
| **Total** | **36** |

If the number of encounters changes, the points per encounter are adjusted to keep the total (the calculator prints the sum and flags it).

### Suggestions for the owner (not applied)
- **Long Shot** (+0.15x per rank) gives the rifle +39% at long range, against +60% for Point Blank and +75% on heads for Deadeye with Marksman. +0.2x per rank would level them.
- **Free skill reset at any time** removes the build choice the point budget is there to create. Suggested: reset only at portals, or keep it as a test option.
- **Chest contents** of the test arena are still written in code (`GBTestArena.cpp`); they move to data when real levels exist.

### Values moved from code to data in v0.26
Enemy AI fractions, projectile lifetime and knockback lift, grenade blast falloff and bounce, the fire wall's touch margin, the ice shield's block arc, player acceleration and friction, what a respawn and an era travel restore, and what a failed QTE costs. All keep their old value; the list is in the day entry.

# Task: move the last hardcoded gameplay numbers into the data files

Project: **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++), in `Glyphbound/`. Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `Prompts/implementer_rules.md` first. In this task you **do not build, do not run tests, do not open the Unreal Editor and do not commit**: the architect reviews your diff, asks a second reviewer, and then builds once. So write code that compiles: check every engine API you are not sure about in `C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9\Engine\Source`.

Owner's request (translated): "Try to leave not a single hardcoded value, because we are going to test all these values and they must be easy to change." The testers change numbers in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` and run `gb.ReloadData` in the console; nothing may need a recompile.

An audit found the literals below. For each one: read it from the data with the helper that file already uses (`UGBDataSubsystem::GetGameValue`, `UGBStatsComponent::GetStat`, the player's `ReadStat`, the QTE component's `ReadStat`, `ReadItemStat`, `FGBSpellCast::Get` / `AGBSpellActor::ReadParam`), keep **today's value as the code default**, and **add the key with today's value to the JSON file**, so the game plays exactly the same after your change. Line numbers are from today's tree and may be off by a few lines.

**Rules for the JSON files:** the architect has just rebalanced them. **Only add new keys. Never change, reorder or reformat an existing value or line.** Edit them as text (keep tabs and the one-line objects as they are). New stat-name constants go next to the similar ones (`Core/GBStatNames.h` or the header that already holds that file's names).

Read the value where it is used (live), not once in a constructor or `BeginPlay`, unless reading it per frame would be wasteful; in that case re-read it on `OnDataReloaded`.

For the six restore keys: on respawn the player is set to `fraction x max` (a respawn always starts from that value); on era travel health and mana are raised to **at least** `fraction x max` and never lowered. `...RefillAmmo` is 1 = refill every gun as today, 0 = leave the ammo as it is. If today's code is one shared "restore everything" function, give it parameters instead of copying it.

Also: `ElementalTickInterval` is read once in `BeginPlay` (`Combat/GBElementalStatusComponent.cpp:53`), so `gb.ReloadData` does not change it. Make it follow a reload.

## 3. Enemies: one new optional stat
`ProjectileGravity` (default 0) for enemy shots, read next to `ProjectileSpeed` / `ProjectileRadius` in `AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:768-773` and passed to the projectile. Add `"ProjectileGravity": 0` to every enemy in `Enemies.json` whose `AttackMode` is `Ranged`.

## 4. Electric grenade (`Items.json`, `ElectricGrenade` "Stats"; code in `Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp:260-280`, `UseThrowable`)
Today the grenade always uses linear blast falloff, never pushes, and bounces with the engine defaults. Read and add: `ExplosionFalloff` 1 (1 = linear falloff, 0 = full damage in the whole radius, same meaning as the spell param), `ExplosionKnockback` 0, `Bounciness` and `Friction` (today's values are the `UProjectileMovementComponent` defaults: look them up in the engine source and write those numbers). `Range` is already in the file.

## 5. Spells (`Spells.json` "Params", each `{ "Base": x, "Min": x, "Max": x, "ScaledBy": "None" }`)
- `TouchMargin` 15 on `FireWall` and `IceWall`: `Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:18` `WallTouchMarginCm` (used near :141).
- `BlockArcDegrees` 180 on `IceShield`: `Spells/GBSpell_Shield.cpp:22` blocks when `Dot >= 0`, i.e. a 180 degree arc in front. Block when the angle between the shield's forward and the direction to the attacker is at most half this arc (compare the dot with `cos(arc/2)`; 180 must behave exactly as today, 360 blocks from every side).

## 6. QTE (`QTE.json` "Stats"; code in `Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.cpp` near :426-507)
Today a spell that fizzles (every QTE step missed) still costs its full mana and starts its full cooldown. Add:
- `QTE.FailManaRefund` 0: share of the mana cost given back when the spell fizzles (0 = today, 1 = full refund).
- `QTE.FailCooldownMult` 1: the cooldown of a fizzled spell is multiplied by this (1 = today, 0 = no cooldown).
Do not change what happens when the QTE itself refuses to start (that refund stays).

    | Ice floor | Deep Freeze | +5 percentage points of slow, with a cap so enemies are never fully stopped |
  - **Weapon tree:** damage, fire rate, **recoil** (better aim), **damage by distance** (shotgun stronger up close, rifle stronger far away), **headshot damage**, reload speed, healing on precise kills, enchant build-up. Layout in Â§18 "Weapon tree".
- **One shared pool of skill points** for both trees.
- **Perks can be reset** (owner, 2026-11-12, to test them): a "Reset skills" option on the skill tree screen removes every unlocked node and gives back all the points spent on them. Provisional details in section 18.
- **Skill points** are given at specific points, mainly for defeating enemies:
  - defeating each **sub-boss**;
  - defeating each **boss**;
  - **clearing a wave** of enemies.

## 6. Magic (4 elements + weapon enchant)
Each element has 3 entries. Air is passives only; the other three are active spells.

| Element | 1 | 2 | 3 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Fire** | Fireball (projectile; **always explodes with a shockwave**, on an enemy, on the world or at the end of its range) | Fire wall | Flamethrower (sustained DPS; **runs while the spell key is held**, until it is released or the mana runs out; **a strong hit interrupts it**) |
| **Lightning** | Lightning bolt (projectile) | Lightning rune trap on the ground; triggers when an enemy walks over it | Static-electricity ball: moves and damages every enemy around it |
| **Ice** | Ice shield (**summoned by the spell, then raised by holding the Block key**, like the shield of a first-person RPG; it only protects while raised; cooldown and limits) | Ice wall | Ice floor: slows enemies, light crowd control |
| **Air** (passives) | Double jump | Improved dash | Increased movement speed |

### Spell loadout
- The player has **5 spells equipped** at a time and can **equip and unequip** them freely.
- **Air passives are always active**: they are not equipped and do not take a slot.
- **Starting spells:** Fireball (very early tier), Lightning bolt (early tier), Ice wall.

### Learning the other spells: elemental zones
- The **medieval castle** has **elemental zones**, one per element.
- Each zone has a **challenge**: a wave, a sub-boss or a boss.
- After the challenge the player gets the element's **knowledge** (from a chest, a book or similar) and **unlocks the missing spells of that element** (fire: fire wall + flamethrower; lightning: rune trap + static ball; ice: ice shield + ice floor).
- **Air passives** are learned the same way, in the air zone.
- The spell tree then upgrades what the player knows.
- **Spells can only be cast with the staff in hand.** With a gun in hand, the only spell available is the weapon enchant. To cast, the player switches to the staff mid-fight.
- Keys: see Â§9.

### Ice shield (owner, 2026-11-12)
- Casting it (with its QTE) **summons** the shield for a time. It is not a force field in front of the player.
- The player **raises it by holding a secondary key** (Block) and lowers it by letting go. It protects from the front **only while raised**.
- Provisional details (architect): Block is **Q** on keyboard and **L2** on gamepad; it needs the staff or the sword in hand (with a gun, L2 is aim); while raised the player moves slower and cannot attack or cast; the shield has health and breaks when it runs out; other spells can be cast while it is summoned and lowered.

### Weapon enchant (always equipped)
- A spell that **cannot be unequipped**.
- **Only usable with a gun in hand** (it enchants the gun).
- Animation: the **left hand draws a magic glyph over the weapon held in the right hand**.
- Effect: the weapon is **temporarily buffed with magic** of one element.
- **Element:** chosen by the player when equipping the spell.
- **Uses the QTE** like every other spell (Â§7).
- **Does not work on the elemental sword.**
- Duration, cost and exact buff per element: **TBD**.

### Elemental build-up (owner's spec, 2026-11-12)
Every enemy has three independent bars: **fire, lightning, ice**. A valid hit of an element fills its bar; a full bar starts that element's status effect.
- **Rules for each bar:** its own threshold, duration, immunity time and decay. After a while without new build-up the bar drains little by little. When the status starts the bar goes back to zero; while the status runs that element does not build up again; after it ends there can be an immunity time before it builds up again. The three bars never affect each other. No reactions between elements yet.
- **Ignite (fire, sustained damage):** fire damage at fixed time steps for the status duration. Sources: fireball, fire wall, flamethrower (on its 0.3 s damage ticks), the ember staff, the elemental sword and a fire-enchanted gun.
- **Shock (lightning, interruption):** an electric discharge (a small hit of lightning damage) and the enemy's reaction: **stun** (stops moving and attacking for the duration, its attack is cancelled), **interrupt** (only cancels the attack it was winding up) or **none**. Sources: lightning bolt (every enemy it passes through, once each), lightning rune (enemies in its blast radius, so Expanded Sigil applies), static orb, the elemental sword and a lightning-enchanted gun.

#include "Spells/GBSpellActor.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/OverlapResult.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "EngineUtils.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

namespace
{
	// Pure geometry, not gameplay: how far the aimed point is pulled back from a wall, and how high above a point
	// the ground search starts (so a slope or a step under the aimed point is still found).
	constexpr float SpellActorAimPullBackCm = 20.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorGroundLiftCm = 100.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorMinTickInterval = 0.05f;
}

AGBSpellActor::AGBSpellActor()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;
	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;
}

void AGBSpellActor::InitSpell(const FGBSpellCast& InCast)
{
	SpellCast = InCast;
	bSpellInitialized = true;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		TryStartSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (!bSpellInitialized)
	{
		// Spawned without the spellbook (placed in a level, or InitSpell will come right after FinishSpawning).
		return;
	}
	TryStartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::TryStartSpell()
{
	if (bSpellStarted || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bSpellStarted = true;
	StartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::OnCastReleased()
{
	EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpell()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bEnded = true;
	GetWorldTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	Destroy();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Destroyed by something else (level unload, era swap): the spellbook must still hear that the spell is over.
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		bEnded = true;
		OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	}
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		World->GetTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

bool AGBSpellActor::FindGround(const FVector& From, FVector& OutGround, float MaxDistance) const
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return false;
	}
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellFindGround), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	const FVector End = From - FVector::UpVector * MaxDistance;
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, End, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		OutGround = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

float AGBSpellActor::ReadParam(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (const float* Value = SpellCast.Values.Find(Param))
	{
		return *Value;
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s' (%s): param '%s' is missing in Spells.json, using %.2f."),
		*SpellCast.SpellId.ToString(), *GetClass()->GetName(), *Param.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	return DefaultValue;
}

FLinearColor AGBSpellActor::GetSpellColor() const
{
	const FLinearColor ElementColor = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(SpellCast.Element));
	return UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color), ElementColor);
}

FName AGBSpellActor::GetSpriteId() const
{
	const FString Sprite = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Sprite);
	return Sprite.IsEmpty() ? NAME_None : FName(*Sprite);
}

void AGBSpellActor::SetupSpellSprite(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, float DefaultHeight) const
{
	if (!Billboard)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (SpriteId.IsNone() || !Library)
	{
		Billboard->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		Billboard->SetVisibility(false);
		return;
	}
	Library->SetupBillboard(Billboard, SpriteId, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, DefaultHeight));
}

void AGBSpellActor::GetCasterAim(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutDirection) const
{
	OutOrigin = SpellCast.Origin;
	OutDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Caster))
	{
		OutOrigin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
		OutDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}
	else if (Caster)
	{
		FRotator EyeRotation;
		Caster->GetActorEyesViewPoint(OutOrigin, EyeRotation);
		OutDirection = EyeRotation.Vector();
	}
	if (OutDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		OutDirection = FVector::ForwardVector;
	}
}

FVector AGBSpellActor::FindAimedGroundPoint(float MaxRange) const
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	FVector Fallback = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	if (Caster)
	{
		Fallback.Z -= Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight();
	}

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return Fallback;
	}

	const FVector Dir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	FVector Point = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (MaxRange > 0.f && !Dir.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellAim), false, this);
		if (Caster)
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		const FVector End = SpellCast.Origin + Dir * MaxRange;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, End, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Point = Hit.ImpactPoint - Dir * SpellActorAimPullBackCm;
		}
		else
		{
			Point = End;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		return FindGround(Fallback + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Point) ? Point : Fallback;
	}

	FVector Ground;
	if (FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Ground))
	{
		return Ground;
	}
	return Fallback;
}

bool AGBSpellActor::IsHostileTarget(const AActor* Actor) const
{
	if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == GetCaster())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(GetCaster(), Actor);
}

void AGBSpellActor::QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellArea), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Center, Rotation, ObjectParams, Shape, Params);
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (IsHostileTarget(Actor))
		{
			OutActors.AddUnique(Actor);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f || Height <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector BoxCenter = GroundCenter + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Height * 0.5f);
	QueryHostiles(BoxCenter, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(FVector(Radius, Radius, Height * 0.5f)), OutActors);
	// The box is only the broad phase: keep actors whose collision touches the disc horizontally.
	OutActors.RemoveAll([&GroundCenter, Radius](const AActor* Actor)
	{
		return FVector::Dist2D(Actor->GetActorLocation(), GroundCenter) > Radius + Actor->GetSimpleCollisionRadius();
	});
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Extent.GetMin() <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, Rotation, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(Extent), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpellAfter(float Seconds)
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell);
	}
	else
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(EndTimer, this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell, Seconds, false);
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::DamageTarget(AActor* Target, float Amount, const FVector& SourceLocation)
{
	if (!Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
	Info.Amount = Amount;
	Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
	Info.HitDirection = (Target->GetActorLocation() - SourceLocation).GetSafeNormal();
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
}

void AGBSpellActor::ApplyOnHitEffect(AActor* Target) const
{
	const FString EffectId = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect);
	if (!Target || EffectId.IsEmpty())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
	{
		Effects->ApplyEffect(FName(*EffectId), GetCaster(),
			SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f), SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f));
	}
}

float AGBSpellActor::SafeInterval(float Interval)
{
	return FMath::Max(Interval, SpellActorMinTickInterval);
}

float AGBSpellActor::ComputeGroundFigureZ(const FVector& GroundPoint, float Radius, const TArray<FVector4>& Existing, float Lift, float LayerStep, float MaxRise)
{
	float Top = GroundPoint.Z + Lift;
	for (const FVector4& Entry : Existing)
	{
		const float Dx = Entry.X - GroundPoint.X;
		const float Dy = Entry.Y - GroundPoint.Y;
		if (FMath::Sqrt(Dx * Dx + Dy * Dy) >= Radius + Entry.W)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (FMath::Abs(Entry.Z - GroundPoint.Z) > MaxRise)
		{
			continue;
		}
		Top = FMath::Max(Top, Entry.Z + LayerStep);
	}
	return Top;
}

void AGBSpellActor::PlaceGroundFigure(UStaticMeshComponent* Figure, float Radius)
{
	if (!Figure)
	{
		return;
	}
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float Lift = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGroundFigure::Lift, 3.f) : 3.f;
	const float LayerStep = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGroundFigure::LayerStep, 1.5f) : 1.5f;
	const float MaxRise = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGroundFigure::LayerMaxRise, 60.f) : 60.f;

	const FVector GroundPoint = GetActorLocation();
	TArray<FVector4> Existing;
	for (TActorIterator<AGBSpellActor> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		AGBSpellActor* Other = *It;
		if (!Other || Other == this || !Other->bHasGroundFigure || Other->bEnded)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (Other->IsPendingKillPending())
		{
			continue;
		}
		Existing.Add(FVector4(Other->GetActorLocation().X, Other->GetActorLocation().Y, Other->GroundFigureZ, Other->GroundFigureRadius));
	}

	const float FigureZ = ComputeGroundFigureZ(GroundPoint, Radius, Existing, Lift, LayerStep, MaxRise);
	Figure->SetWorldLocation(FVector(GroundPoint.X, GroundPoint.Y, FigureZ));

	GroundFigureRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, Radius);
	GroundFigureZ = FigureZ;
	bHasGroundFigure = true;
}

bool AGBStaff::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const float ManaCost = GetStat(GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f);
	if (ManaCost > 0.f)
	{
		UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes();
		if (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(ManaCost))
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector Direction = FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle;
	if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = MakeDamageInfo();
	Config.HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 10.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Config.Damage.Element));
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Sprite = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::ProjectileSprite))
		{
			Config.SpriteId = FName(**Sprite);
		}
	}

	return AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player) != nullptr;
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::PerformAttack()
{
	AActor* Goal = Target.Get();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (!Goal || !Def || !GetWorld())
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Damage = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);

	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Damage;
	Info.Element = Def->Element;
	Info.Instigator = this;
	Info.Causer = this;
	Info.SourceId = EnemyId;
	Info.OnHitEffect = FName(*Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::OnHitEffect));

	const FName Mode(*Def->AttackMode);
	if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeMelee)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Tolerance = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::MeleeReachTolerance, 1.2f) : 1.2f;
		const FVector ToGoal = Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation();
		if (ToGoal.Size() <= AttackRange * Tolerance && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			Info.HitLocation = Goal->GetActorLocation();
			Info.HitDirection = ToGoal.GetSafeNormal();
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Goal, Info);
		}
	}
	else if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged)
	{
		FGBProjectileConfig Config;
		Config.Damage = Info;
		Config.Damage.Causer = nullptr;
		Config.Speed = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, Config.Speed);
		Config.Radius = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
		Config.GravityScale = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
		Config.MaxRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f) * GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileRangeMult, 1.5f);
		Config.MaxLifetime = GetGameValue(GBProjectileKey::ProjectileMaxLifetime, Config.MaxLifetime);
		Config.bPixelArt = Def->bPixelArt;
		if (Def->bPixelArt)
		{
			const FString SpriteId = Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::ProjectileSprite);
			Config.SpriteId = FName(SpriteId.IsEmpty() ? GBEnemyCharacterLocal::DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite : *SpriteId);
		}
		else
		{
			Config.Color = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor),
				GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)));
		}

		const FVector Forward = GetActorForwardVector();
		const FVector Origin = GetChestLocation() + Forward * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() + Config.Radius);
		FVector Direction = Goal->GetActorLocation() - Origin;
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			Direction = Forward;
		}
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(GetWorld(), Config, Origin, Direction, this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown AttackMode '%s'"), *EnemyId.ToString(), *Def->AttackMode);
	}
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
{
	const float HalfHeight = GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
	const float FeetZ = GetActorLocation().Z - HalfHeight;
	return UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(HitLocation.Z, FeetZ, 2.f * HalfHeight, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::HeadFraction, 0.f));
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		bProvoked = true;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged()
{
	ResetStuck();
	if (!EraComponent->IsPresent() && State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		StopMoving();
		bRepositioning = false;
		if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	WallAttributes->InitializeFromStats();
	WallAttributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	// Not inside the damage call that killed it.
	EndSpellAfter(0.f);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	// Pawns pressed against a blocking wall still count as touching it for damage ticks.
	const float TouchMargin = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::TouchMargin, 15.f);
	const FVector Extent = Box->GetUnscaledBoxExtent() + FVector(TouchMargin);
	GatherHostilesInBox(GetActorLocation(), GetActorQuat(), Extent, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}

- **Ignite (fire, sustained damage):** fire damage at fixed time steps for the status duration. Sources: fireball, fire wall, flamethrower (on its 0.3 s damage ticks), the ember staff, the elemental sword and a fire-enchanted gun.
- **Shock (lightning, interruption):** an electric discharge (a small hit of lightning damage) and the enemy's reaction: **stun** (stops moving and attacking for the duration, its attack is cancelled), **interrupt** (only cancels the attack it was winding up) or **none**. Sources: lightning bolt (every enemy it passes through, once each), lightning rune (enemies in its blast radius, so Expanded Sigil applies), static orb, the elemental sword and a lightning-enchanted gun.
- **Frost (ice, slow and control):** the enemy's reaction: **freeze** (cannot move or attack for the duration), **slow** (a strong slow) or **none**. Sources: the **ice floor only** among spells (low build-up on each of its ticks, so standing on it fills the bar slowly and an ice-enchanted gun finishes it faster), the elemental sword and an ice-enchanted gun. The ice wall and the ice shield build up nothing. The ice floor's own slow (50%, Deep Freeze 55/60/65%, cap 80%, QTE) is unchanged. **Slows never stack:** when the ice floor and Frost both slow an enemy, only the strongest counts.
- **Per enemy (no global multipliers by enemy type):** each enemy can set, per element, its damage taken, its build-up taken (separately, so they balance apart), its threshold, the status duration, the immunity time, the decay, and its reaction (or immunity to the status: an enemy can be immune to Shock and still take lightning damage). Values not set by an enemy fall back to defaults in `Game.json`.
- **Per source:** each spell has its own build-up per hit or per tick (`BuildUp` in `Spells.json`); each weapon has its own build-up per element (`FireBuildUp`, `LightningBuildUp`, `IceBuildUp` in `Weapons.json`). An unenchanted gun builds up nothing; the apprentice staff (arcane) builds up nothing. One shot counts once per enemy: the eight pellets of a shotgun shot give one build-up to each enemy they hit.
- **Weak point bonus:** a weapon hit on the head or on a weak point multiplies its build-up (`WeakPointBuildUpMult`), only once even if the head is also a weak point. It does not change the head damage bonus.
- **Visuals:** not decided yet (Niagara, shaders or something per enemy). The code only sends events (build-up changed, status started, status ended) that each enemy's look can use later. A debug view (`gb.ShowElemental 1` in the console) prints the bars over enemies for testing; it is not the game's indicator.
- **Removed by the owner (2026-11-12):** the small effects every elemental hit used to apply (burn on fire hits, the 40% slow on lightning hits, the enchant's chill). The bar's status effect is now the only elemental effect. The electric grenade builds up lightning instead of applying the old shock (architect).

### General
- Spells cost **mana**.
- **Health and mana regenerate on their own** (potions and the injection speed it up). **No stamina.**
- Numbers for each spell (damage, cost, cooldowns, upgrade tiers): **TBD**, given later.
- The ice shield's exact limits (duration, damage absorbed, break behavior): **TBD**.

FString AGBHeldItem::GetDisplayName() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : WeaponId.ToString();
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	float Base = DefaultValue;
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
		{
			Base = *Value;
		}
	}

	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(Slot, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
	}
	return Base;
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
{
	bEquipped = true;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnUnequipped()
{
	bEquipped = false;
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

EGBHudSkin AGBHeldItem::GetHudSkin() const
{
	return Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetTimeSinceLastAttack() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? static_cast<float>(World->GetTimeSeconds() - LastAttackTime) : 1000.f;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();

		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Chase:
		// Line of sight for melee too: an orc on the other side of a low wall keeps going around it instead of swinging at stone.
		if (bCanAttack && Distance <= AttackRange && HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			StopMoving();
			FaceTowards(Goal);
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Windup);
		}
		else
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Windup:
		// A shot needs a clear line: the player stepped behind cover, so reposition instead of shooting the wall.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			break;
		}
		FaceTowards(Goal);
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackWindup, 0.f))
		{
			PerformAttack();
			if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
			{
				SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
			}
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Recover:
		// Ranged enemies do not wait out the interval behind a wall: they move until they see the player again.
		if (IsRanged() && !HasLineOfSight(Goal))
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
			bRepositioning = true;
		}
		else
		{
			if (bRepositioning)
			{
				StopMoving();
				bRepositioning = false;
			}
			FaceTowards(Goal);
		}
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackInterval, 0.f))
		{
			bRepositioning = false;
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}

	UpdateStuck(DeltaSeconds);
}


void AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Data->GetPlayer().Stats);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	UpdateFocusedInteractable();

	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		// GDD 7: the movement keys are busy with the QTE, so the last movement input is held.
		if (CanUseGameplayInput() && !bDashing && !LastMoveInput.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			ApplyMoveInput(LastMoveInput);
		}
	}
	else if (GFrameCounter > LastMoveInputFrame + 1)
	{
		// No Move event for a frame: the stick / keys were released (also covers releases during a QTE).
		LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	}

	if (bDashing)
	{
		DashTimeLeft -= DeltaSeconds;
		if (DashTimeLeft <= 0.f)
		{
			EndDash();
		}
		else if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
		{
			MoveComp->Velocity = DashVelocity;
		}
	}

	TickRecoil(DeltaSeconds);
	TickViewShake(DeltaSeconds);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stats and movement

float AGBPlayerCharacter::ReadStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return DefaultValue;
	}
	if (!Stats->HasBaseStat(Stat) && !GWarnedMissingPlayerStats.Contains(Stat))
	{
		GWarnedMissingPlayerStats.Add(Stat);
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: stat '%s' is missing from Player.json, using %.2f."), *Stat.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	}
	return Stats->GetStat(Stat, DefaultValue);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::ApplyMovementStats()
{
	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (!MoveComp)
	{
		return;
	}

	UpdateWalkSpeed();
	MoveComp->JumpZVelocity = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBStat::JumpVelocity, 620.f));
	MoveComp->AirControl = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBStat::AirControl, 0.f));
	MoveComp->GravityScale = ReadStat(GBStat::GravityScale, 1.6f);
	// Committed jumps (GDD 18): no air steering, no air braking or lateral friction, so the takeoff keeps its speed.
	MoveComp->BrakingDecelerationFalling = 0.f;

#include "Spells/GBSpell_Orb.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"

#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	/** Visual only: how bright the area sphere is compared with the spell color. */
	constexpr float OrbAreaBrightness = 0.15f;
}

AGBSpell_Orb::AGBSpell_Orb()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);

	Area = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Area"));
	Area->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Area->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Area->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Area->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::StartSpell()
{
	MoveSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 400.f));
	RangeLeft = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 2000.f));
	OrbRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 250.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 6.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 20.f)) * Interval;

	MoveDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	if (MoveDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		MoveDirection = GetActorForwardVector();
	}
	FVector Start = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Start = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	SetActorLocation(Start);

	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 60.f);
	if (SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ShowArea, 1.f) > 0.5f)
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Area, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(OrbRadius * 2.f), GetSpellColor() * OrbAreaBrightness, true);
	}
	else
	{
		Area->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	bMoving = MoveSpeed > 0.f && RangeLeft > 0.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(bMoving);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bMoving || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Step = FMath::Min(MoveSpeed * DeltaSeconds, RangeLeft);
	const FVector From = GetActorLocation();
	const FVector To = From + MoveDirection * Step;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellOrbMove), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, To, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		// Stops at the wall and keeps crackling there until its time runs out.
		SetActorLocation(Hit.Location);
		bMoving = false;
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		return;
	}

	SetActorLocation(To);
	RangeLeft -= Step;
	if (RangeLeft <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		EndSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick()
{
	if (DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesInSphere(GetActorLocation(), OrbRadius, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}
