1. **[A] [high] Build damage is substantially underestimated — `balance_model.js`, `BuildMult` and boss-stage multipliers.** Replace flat **1.3–1.75×** with calculated builds. Max Heavy Rounds, Quick Trigger, Rapid Reload, Marksman and Deadeye cost **18 points**: pistol head multiplier is **3.5×**, interval **0.19 s**, reload **0.84 s**. At QTE 0.75, enchant is **1.30×**: approximately **117 expected DPS**, or **146 against a weakness**, before spells. Swapping Deadeye for Point Blank gives approximately **177/221 shotgun DPS** at close range. These imply roughly **68/45 seconds** against the future Valkyrie while ammunition lasts, not two minutes. Keep perk percentages pending this correction.

2. **[A] [high] “Ammo cannot kill a boss” is false for precision builds — GDD §19.** Change that assertion to a build-dependent resource table. The pistol build above carries **11,645 expected damage** against a weakness at 75% accuracy with every hit a weak-point hit: enough for either Valkyrie, approximately **29–32 seconds of modelled combat**, plus casting overhead. At 25% head share it carries only **5,407**. Weak-point accuracy radically changes both duration and resource sufficiency; the separate **1.5× build-up bonus must not multiply damage**.

3. **[A] [high] Boss modelling assumes ammunition continues indefinitely — `balance_model.js`, boss-health calculation.** Change gun uptime after carried ammunition is exhausted from **70% to 0%**, switching to the actual staff/sword and accounting for drops. At the model’s unmodified accuracy/head share, carried damage is only about **1,969 pistol / 2,025 shotgun / 1,677 rifle**. Free weapons prevent a resource softlock, but a mage with a staff can take much longer than the advertised boss times.

4. **[A] [high] Permanent defensive coverage is available without perfect QTE — `Spells.json`, cooldowns.** Propose **IceWall 12→16 s; IceShield 18→34 s** for Base/Min/Max. With Quick Recall III, current cooldowns are **8.4/12.6 s**; at QTE 0.75, wall duration is **9.5 s**, and Enduring Guard III makes shield duration **20.3 s**. Frugal Casting reduces each cast to **14 mana**, easily sustainable. These changes create individual downtime even at maximum duration; alternating defenses still needs testing.

5. **[A] [high] Ice Floor’s slow lasts four seconds after application — `Spells.json`, `IceFloor.EffectDuration`; `StatusEffects.json`, `Slow.Duration`.** Change **EffectDuration 1→0.25** and **Cooldown 10→14 s**. `ApplyEffect` treats EffectDuration as a multiplier: currently **4×1=4 seconds**, not one. At QTE 0.75, seven seconds of floor plus the lingering slow already exceeds its unmodified cooldown; Quick Recall worsens this. Deep Freeze III also reaches **72.5% slow** at that score and bypasses bosses’ 25% elemental-Frost limit.

6. **[A] [high] Real-time QTE cost is absent — `balance_model.js`, `ASSUME`; `QTE.json`.** Add a provisional **2 real seconds per cast**, replacing the implicit zero-duration cast assumption; retain dilation **0.1**. Forty casts add approximately **72 seconds beyond elapsed game time**. A generic 70% attack uptime cannot simultaneously establish real fight length, mana regeneration and cooldown recovery under this slowdown.

7. **[A] [medium] Mana regeneration is not a fixed 70% duty cycle — `balance_model.js`, mana/spell DPS.** Replace **0.7** with cast-schedule-derived regeneration. A 15-mana cast every three game seconds allows only **9 mana regeneration** between casts, draining the initial bar in roughly **50 seconds**. Sustainable spacing is **4 seconds**, or **3.25 seconds with Frugal Casting III**. Continuous casting at intervals ≤1.5 seconds yields no ordinary regeneration.

8. **[A] [medium] Enemy DPS omits windup — `balance_model.js`, enemy and encounter calculations; `GBEnemyCharacter.cpp`, Windup/Recover.** Change denominator **AttackInterval → AttackInterval + AttackWindup**. Future Valkyrie raw DPS becomes **18→12.4**, and expected DPS **7.2→4.97**; nominal survival becomes **14→20 seconds**. Melee bosses additionally stop to wind up while the faster player escapes, making sustained kiting and regeneration much safer than the model suggests.

9. **[A] [medium] Ammo drops can produce a surplus — `Enemies.json`, `Orc_Future.Drops[Ammo_Magazine].Chance`.** Propose **0.20→0.08**. A specialized enchanted pistol kills this 70-HP soldier with one head hit; at 75% accuracy, the current drop returns **three rounds per kill versus approximately 1.33 spent**. The proposed chance returns **1.2 rounds**. Update the economy model to use weapon-specific accuracy, discrete shots, overkill, perks and headshots before adjusting other drops.

10. **[A] [medium] Several waves miss the approved minimum even in the current calculator — `Encounters.json`, `Wave_*_1/2`.** As a provisional adjustment, increase **four sub-waves→five** by repeating each encounter’s final sub-wave. Current estimates **48/49/56/56 seconds** become approximately **62/63/71/72 seconds**, without increasing individual enemy health or simultaneous crowd size. Recalculate after fixing build and QTE modelling.

11. **[A] [medium] Persistent Charge can spend points on duration the orb cannot use — `Spells.json`, `StaticOrb.Range`.** Propose Base/Min/Max **2000/1200/2800→2700/1800/3600 cm**. At Base, duration III gives **8.7 seconds**, but range ends unobstructed flight after **6.67 seconds**; at perfect QTE, **11.6 seconds** is cut to **9.33**. The proposed ranges accommodate the full perk duration.

12. **[B] [medium] Staff projectiles still ignore the lifetime setting — `Weapons/GBStaff.cpp`, `PerformAttack`; `Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.cpp`, `StartSpell`.** Replace the staff’s untouched **10-second config default** with a `ProjectileMaxLifetime` lookup. Also move the spell’s lookup outside `MaxRange > 0`; otherwise setting range to zero silently disables lifetime tuning.

13. **[B] [medium] Active wall/shield tuning does not refresh — `GBSpell_Shield.cpp`, `StartSpell`; `GBSpellActor.cpp`, `ReadParam`.** Replace cast-only caching of **BlockArcDegrees=180** and **TouchMargin=15** with reload-aware values. `ReadParam` reads the captured `SpellCast.Values`, so even the wall’s per-tick call retains the old value; currently testers must recast.

14. **[A] [low] The budget works, but its documentation is wrong — GDD §19, Skill points.** Change weapon/tree totals **33/75→36/78**, and **48%→46%**. A concrete 36-point hybrid is **18 points** in the pistol build above, **3 Point Blank**, **6 Frugal Casting**, and **3 each Fireball Damage, Wide Blast and Overcharge**: strong magic plus two substantially upgraded guns.

Checked and found fine: 36 awarded points, basic enemy kill counts, moderate resistance multipliers, post-status immunity, Precision Recovery’s maximum **0.75 HP/s**, nonstacking potions, grenade engine defaults, added key names and elemental tick reload; no definite compile blocker found by inspection. No files changed, build run or Unreal launched.
